← Model Evals

Review progress

90 / 2031 model–benchmark pairs reviewed across 124 models.

Each comparison belongs to one publisher report. Benchmark-maintainer evaluations are reviewed separately for the exact version, subset and metric. “Not yet reviewed” does not mean a result is unavailable. Source verification checks transcription and evaluation scope; it does not reproduce the experiment.

Reviewed includes documented unresolved sources and incompatible setups; these never enter the official ranking. Models are complete only after source coverage and every collected metric have been reviewed. 1135 catalog-only identities have no collected results and are not counted as reviewed.

October 24 Kimi audit and complete official snapshots ↗
